LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "a0"."Id", "a0"."UserName"
    FROM "AspNetUsers" AS "a0"
    WHERE "a0"."IsDeleted" = 0
) AS "a1" ON "a"."UserId" = "a1"."Id"
ORDER BY "a"."Timestamp" DESC
